  "content": "# SANDMAN.md\n\nComplete deployment, exploration, inspection, and debugging guide for the Sandcastle installation on **sandman** (100.106.185.92).\n\n## Table of Contents\n\n- [Deployment](#deployment)\n- [Explore](#explore)\n- [Inspect](#inspect)\n- [Debug](#debug)\n\n---\n\n## Deployment\n\n### Prerequisites\n\n- SSH access: `ssh thies@100.106.185.92`\n- Tailscale connectivity to sandman\n- GitHub Container Registry access for `ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle`\n\n### Initial Bootstrap\n\nIf setting up sandman from scratch:\n\n```bash\nssh thies@100.106.185.92\n\n# Clone repository\ngit clone https://github.com/thieso2/sandcastle.git /opt/sandcastle\ncd /opt/sandcastle\n\n# Run bootstrap script (installs Docker, Sysbox, Caddy, UFW)\nsudo bash bootstrap/sandcastle-bootstrap.sh\n\n# Run installer\nsudo bash installer.sh install\n```\n\nThe installer will:\n- Create `$SANDCASTLE_HOME` directory structure (default: `/opt/sandcastle`)\n- Set up Dockyard (isolated Docker daemon with sysbox-runc)\n- Generate database password and save to `/opt/sandcastle/data/postgres/.secrets`\n- Create PostgreSQL init script for 4 databases\n- Configure Traefik proxy\n- Generate environment files\n\n### Deploy/Update Application\n\n```bash\nssh thies@100.106.185.92\ncd /opt/sandcastle\n\n# Pull latest changes\ngit pull origin main\n\n# Pull latest images\ndocker-compose pull\n\n# Restart services\ndocker-compose up -d\n\n# Run migrations (if needed)\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails db:migrate\n\n# Check status\ndocker-compose ps\n```\n\n### Environment Variables\n\nKey environment variables (set in `.env` or `docker-compose.yml`):\n\n```bash\nSECRET_KEY_BASE=<generated>\nRAILS_MASTER_KEY=<from config/master.key>\nSANDCASTLE_HOST=sandman.example.com\nSANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR=/data\nPOSTGRES_PASSWORD=<from /opt/sandcastle/data/postgres/.secrets>\n```\n\n### Registry Authentication\n\nIf deploying from private registry:\n\n```bash\necho $GITHUB_TOKEN | docker login ghcr.io -u thieso2 --password-stdin\n```\n\n### Reinstall/Reset\n\n```bash\n# Reset (keeps user data, recreates containers)\nsudo bash installer.sh reset\n\n# Full uninstall (removes everything)\nsudo bash installer.sh uninstall\n```\n\n**Important:** Database password is preserved in `/opt/sandcastle/data/postgres/.secrets` across reinstalls to prevent authentication failures with existing data.\n\n---\n\n## Explore\n\n### Directory Structure\n\n```\n/opt/sandcastle/                    # Application root\n├── app/                            # Rails app\n│   ├── controllers/\n│   ├── models/\n│   ├── services/                   # Business logic\n│   │   ├── sandbox_manager.rb\n│   │   ├── terminal_manager.rb\n│   │   ├── tailscale_manager.rb\n│   │   └── system_status.rb\n│   └── views/\n├── vendor/sandcastle-cli/          # Go CLI\n├── images/sandbox/                 # Sandbox Docker image\n├── installer.sh                    # Main installer\n├── install-defaults                # Installer config documentation\n├── docker-compose.yml              # Production deployment\n└── docker/                         # Docker configs\n    └── postgres/\n        └── init-databases.sh       # PostgreSQL multi-DB init\n\n/data/                              # Persistent data (SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR)\n├── postgres/                       # PostgreSQL data\n│   └── .secrets                    # Database password (600 perms)\n├── traefik/\n│   └── dynamic/                    # Terminal forwardAuth configs\n├── users/{username}/\n│   ├── home/                       # User home (bind-mounted to sandboxes)\n│   └── tailscale/                  # Tailscale state\n├── sandboxes/{name}/\n│   └── vol/                        # Persistent workspace volumes\n└── wetty/{user}-{sandbox}/         # WeTTY SSH keypairs\n```\n\n### Key Components\n\n#### 1. Dockyard (Isolated Docker Daemon)\n\n```bash\n# Check Dockyard status\nsudo systemctl status dockyard\n\n# View Dockyard logs\nsudo journalctl -u dockyard -f\n\n# Inspect Dockyard config\ncat /opt/sandcastle/etc/env.dockyard\n```\n\nDockyard is an isolated Docker daemon with:\n- `sysbox-runc` as default runtime\n- `--iptables=false` to avoid conflicts\n- Used for sandbox containers only (app containers use standard Docker)\n\n#### 2. PostgreSQL Databases\n\nFour separate databases:\n\n```bash\n# Connect to PostgreSQL\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres\n\n# List databases\n\\l\n\n# Database layout:\n# - sandcastle_production       → Primary (users, sandboxes, api_tokens)\n# - sandcastle_production_cache → Solid Cache\n# - sandcastle_production_queue → Solid Queue\n# - sandcastle_production_cable → Solid Cable\n```\n\n#### 3. Traefik Proxy\n\n```bash\n# View Traefik logs\ndocker-compose logs -f traefik\n\n# Check dynamic configs (terminal forwardAuth)\nls -la /data/traefik/dynamic/\n\n# Traefik dashboard (if exposed)\ncurl http://localhost:8080/dashboard/\n```\n\nTraefik config includes:\n- `response_timeout: 60` for Tailscale login flow (blocks ~13s)\n- Dynamic terminal configs: `terminal-{id}.yml`\n- ForwardAuth middleware to Rails `/terminal/auth`\n\n#### 4. Sandbox Containers\n\n```bash\n# List all sandboxes (via Dockyard socket)\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker ps\n\n# Or via Rails console\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n> Sandbox.all.map { |s| [s.name, s.status, s.container_id] }\n\n# Inspect a sandbox\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker inspect sc-{sandbox-name}\n```\n\n#### 5. WeTTY Terminal Containers\n\n```bash\n# List terminal containers\ndocker ps --filter \"name=sc-wetty-\"\n\n# View terminal logs\ndocker logs sc-wetty-{user}-{sandbox}\n\n# Inspect SSH keypair\nls -la /data/wetty/{user}-{sandbox}/\n```\n\n#### 6. Tailscale Sidecars\n\n```bash\n# List Tailscale containers\ndocker ps --filter \"name=sc-ts-\"\n\n# Check Tailscale status\ndocker exec sc-ts-{username} tailscale status\n\n# View Tailscale networks\ndocker network ls | grep sc-ts-net\n```\n\n---\n\n## Inspect\n\n### Application Health\n\n```bash\n# Check all services\ndocker-compose ps\n\n# View app logs\ndocker-compose logs -f web\n\n# Check background jobs\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n> SolidQueue::Job.count\n> SolidQueue::RecurringTask.all\n```\n\n### Sandbox Status\n\n```bash\n# Rails console check\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n\n# Get all sandboxes\n> Sandbox.all.pluck(:name, :status, :ssh_port)\n\n# Check Docker sync\n> SandboxManager.new.sync_all_containers\n\n# Manual Docker inspection (via Dockyard)\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker ps -a --filter \"name=sc-\"\n```\n\n### SSH Connectivity\n\n```bash\n# Test SSH to a sandbox\nssh -p 2201 username@100.106.185.92\n\n# Check SSH port mappings\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker ps --format \"table {{.Names}}\\t{{.Ports}}\"\n\n# View active SSH connections\nsudo netstat -tnp | grep :220\n```\n\n### Database Status\n\n```bash\n# Check connection\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres -c \"SELECT version();\"\n\n# Database sizes\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres -c \"\n  SELECT datname, pg_size_pretty(pg_database_size(datname))\n  FROM pg_database\n  WHERE datname LIKE 'sandcastle_%'\n  ORDER BY datname;\n\"\n\n# Active connections\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres -c \"\n  SELECT datname, count(*) FROM pg_stat_activity\n  GROUP BY datname;\n\"\n```\n\n### Traefik Routes\n\n```bash\n# List terminal routes\nls -la /data/traefik/dynamic/ | grep terminal-\n\n# Check route file\ncat /data/traefik/dynamic/terminal-123.yml\n\n# Traefik API (if enabled)\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/http/routers\n```\n\n### Tailscale Connectivity\n\n```bash\n# Check user Tailscale state\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n> User.where(tailscale_state: 'enabled').pluck(:username)\n\n# Inspect sidecar\ndocker exec sc-ts-{username} tailscale status --json | jq\n\n# Check bridge network\ndocker network inspect sc-ts-net-{username}\n\n# Verify subnet routes\ndocker exec sc-ts-{username} tailscale status | grep \"subnet routes\"\n```\n\n### System Resources\n\n```bash\n# Docker stats (Dockyard containers)\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker stats --no-stream\n\n# Disk usage\ndu -sh /data/*\ndf -h\n\n# Check for zombie containers\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker ps -a --filter \"status=exited\"\n```\n\n### Background Job Monitoring\n\n```bash\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n\n# Check recurring jobs\n> SolidQueue::RecurringTask.all.each { |t| puts \"#{t.key}: #{t.next_time}\" }\n\n# Check ContainerSyncJob runs\n> SolidQueue::Job.where(class_name: 'ContainerSyncJob').order(created_at: :desc).limit(10)\n\n# View failed jobs\n> SolidQueue::FailedExecution.last(5)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Debug\n\n### Common Issues\n\n#### 1. Sandbox Won't Start\n\n**Symptoms:** Container status stuck at `creating` or `stopped`\n\n```bash\n# Check Dockyard logs\nsudo journalctl -u dockyard -f\n\n# Inspect container logs (via Dockyard)\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker logs sc-{sandbox-name}\n\n# Check Sysbox runtime\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker info | grep -A5 \"Runtimes:\"\n\n# Verify runc version (must be 1.1.15, NOT 1.2+)\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker exec sc-{sandbox-name} runc --version\n```\n\n**Common causes:**\n- Sysbox not loaded: `sudo systemctl restart sysbox`\n- Port conflict: Check `netstat -tnlp | grep :22XX`\n- Volume mount issues: Check `/data/users/{name}/home` permissions\n\n#### 2. Web Terminal Not Loading\n\n**Symptoms:** Terminal shows \"Connection refused\" or redirect loop\n\n```bash\n# Check WeTTY container\ndocker logs sc-wetty-{user}-{sandbox}\n\n# Verify Traefik config exists\nls -la /data/traefik/dynamic/terminal-*.yml\n\n# Test forwardAuth endpoint\ncurl -i -H \"Cookie: session_id=<session>\" \\\n  -H \"X-Forwarded-Uri: /terminal/123\" \\\n  http://localhost:3000/terminal/auth\n\n# Check SSH key injection\ndocker exec sc-{sandbox-name} cat /home/{user}/.ssh/authorized_keys\n```\n\n**Common causes:**\n- SSH key not appended: `entrypoint.sh` bug (must append, not overwrite)\n- Traefik config race: 1-second sleep in `TerminalManager#open` may be insufficient\n- Network isolation: WeTTY container not on `sandcastle-web` network\n\n#### 3. Tailscale Authentication Stuck\n\n**Symptoms:** User stuck in `pending` state, login URL not working\n\n```bash\n# Check sidecar logs\ndocker logs sc-ts-{username}\n\n# Verify entrypoint override (interactive login)\ndocker inspect sc-ts-{username} | jq '.[0].Config.Entrypoint'\n# Should be: [\"tailscaled\", \"--socket=/var/run/tailscale/tailscaled.sock\", \"--statedir=/var/lib/tailscale\"]\n\n# Check tailscale status\ndocker exec sc-ts-{username} tailscale status --json | jq '.BackendState'\n```\n\n**Common causes:**\n- `containerboot` entrypoint conflict: Must override to `tailscaled` for interactive login\n- Traefik timeout: Increase `response_timeout` (default 60s may be insufficient)\n- State persistence issue: Check `/data/users/{name}/tailscale` permissions\n\n#### 4. Database Connection Failures\n\n**Symptoms:** Rails app shows \"PG::ConnectionBad\"\n\n```bash\n# Check password file\nsudo cat /data/postgres/.secrets\n\n# Verify environment variable\ndocker-compose exec web env | grep POSTGRES_PASSWORD\n\n# Test connection from app container\ndocker-compose exec web psql -h postgres -U postgres -d sandcastle_production\n\n# Check PostgreSQL logs\ndocker-compose logs postgres | tail -50\n```\n\n**Common causes:**\n- Password mismatch: Reinstall without preserving `.secrets` file\n- PostgreSQL not ready: `depends_on` in docker-compose may need health check\n- Multiple databases not created: Check `docker/postgres/init-databases.sh` execution\n\n#### 5. Port Conflicts (SSH 2201-2299)\n\n**Symptoms:** \"Port already in use\" error when creating sandbox\n\n```bash\n# Check allocated ports in DB\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n> Sandbox.where(status: ['running', 'stopped']).pluck(:name, :ssh_port)\n\n# Check actual listening ports\nsudo netstat -tnlp | grep -E ':22[0-9]{2}'\n\n# Find orphaned port bindings\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker ps --format \"{{.Names}}\\t{{.Ports}}\" | grep 22\n\n# Sync DB with Docker reality\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails runner 'SandboxManager.new.sync_all_containers'\n```\n\n**Resolution:**\n- Run `ContainerSyncJob` manually to reconcile state\n- Or manually update DB: `Sandbox.find_by(name: 'foo').update(status: 'destroyed')`\n\n#### 6. Docker Socket Permission Denied\n\n**Symptoms:** App can't connect to Dockyard socket\n\n```bash\n# Check socket permissions\nls -la /var/run/docker-dockyard.sock\n\n# Verify app container group\ndocker-compose exec web id\n# Should include group 988 (Dockyard socket group)\n\n# Check docker-compose.yml\ngrep -A2 \"group_add:\" docker-compose.yml\n# Should have: group_add: [988]\n```\n\n**Resolution:**\n- Ensure `group-add: 988` in `docker-compose.yml`\n- Restart app container: `docker-compose restart web`\n\n#### 7. Entrypoint.sh Issues\n\n**Symptoms:** User not created, SSH keys not injected, Docker-in-Docker not working\n\n```bash\n# Inspect entrypoint execution\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker logs sc-{sandbox-name}\n\n# Check user creation\ndocker exec sc-{sandbox-name} id {username}\n\n# Verify dockerd running\ndocker exec sc-{sandbox-name} docker ps\n\n# Check SSH keys appended (not overwritten)\ndocker exec sc-{sandbox-name} cat /home/{user}/.ssh/authorized_keys | grep -c \"^ssh-rsa\"\n```\n\n**Common causes:**\n- MTU mismatch: `entrypoint.sh` must detect eth0 MTU and configure dockerd\n- SSH key overwrite: Must use `>>` (append), not `>` (overwrite)\n- runc version: 1.2+ breaks Sysbox, must pin to 1.1.15\n\n#### 8. ContainerSyncJob Failures\n\n**Symptoms:** Sandbox status out of sync with Docker reality\n\n```bash\n# Check job failures\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n> SolidQueue::FailedExecution.where(job_class: 'ContainerSyncJob').last(5).each do |f|\n>   puts \"#{f.created_at}: #{f.error}\"\n> end\n\n# Run manual sync\n> SandboxManager.new.sync_all_containers\n\n# Check Solid Queue logs\ndocker-compose logs web | grep ContainerSyncJob\n```\n\n**Resolution:**\n- Ensure Dockyard socket accessible from app container\n- Check for stale container IDs in database\n- Verify `docker-api` gem version compatibility\n\n### Rails Console Debugging\n\n```bash\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n\n# Force sync sandbox state\n> sb = Sandbox.find_by(name: 'test-sandbox')\n> SandboxManager.new.sync_container(sb)\n\n# Check Docker API connectivity\n> require 'docker'\n> Docker.url\n> Docker.info\n\n# Inspect terminal records\n> Terminal.where(status: 'open').each do |t|\n>   puts \"#{t.id}: #{t.sandbox.name} -> wetty container: #{Docker::Container.get(\"sc-wetty-#{t.sandbox.user.username}-#{t.sandbox.name}\").info['State']}\"\n> end\n\n# Check Tailscale sidecar status\n> user = User.find_by(username: 'alice')\n> TailscaleManager.new(user).status\n```\n\n### Log Aggregation\n\n```bash\n# All app logs\ndocker-compose logs -f --tail=100 web\n\n# All Dockyard container logs\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker logs --tail=100 sc-{container-name}\n\n# System journal (Dockyard, Sysbox)\nsudo journalctl -u dockyard -u sysbox -f\n\n# Combined view (requires multitail)\nmultitail \\\n  -l \"docker-compose logs -f web\" \\\n  -l \"sudo journalctl -u dockyard -f\" \\\n  -l \"sudo journalctl -u sysbox -f\"\n```\n\n### Network Debugging\n\n```bash\n# Check sandcastle-web network\ndocker network inspect sandcastle-web\n\n# Verify WeTTY can reach sandbox SSH\ndocker exec sc-wetty-{user}-{sandbox} ping -c3 sc-{sandbox-name}\n\n# Check Tailscale bridge networks\ndocker network ls | grep sc-ts-net\ndocker network inspect sc-ts-net-{username}\n\n# Verify subnet routes advertised\ndocker exec sc-ts-{username} tailscale status | grep \"subnet routes\"\n```\n\n### Emergency Recovery\n\n```bash\n# Stop all services\ndocker-compose down\n\n# Restart Dockyard\nsudo systemctl restart dockyard\n\n# Restart Sysbox\nsudo systemctl restart sysbox\n\n# Clean orphaned containers (DANGEROUS: will lose running sandboxes)\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker container prune -f\n\n# Clean orphaned networks\ndocker network prune -f\n\n# Restart services\ndocker-compose up -d\n\n# Force container sync\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails runner 'SandboxManager.new.sync_all_containers'\n```\n\n### Performance Profiling\n\n```bash\n# Check slow database queries\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres -d sandcastle_production -c \"\n  SELECT query, calls, mean_exec_time, max_exec_time\n  FROM pg_stat_statements\n  ORDER BY mean_exec_time DESC\n  LIMIT 10;\n\"\n\n# Rails request profiling (add rack-mini-profiler gem)\n# Then visit: http://sandman.example.com?pp=help\n\n# Check container resource limits\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker stats --no-stream\n```\n\n---\n\n## Quick Reference\n\n### Essential Commands\n\n```bash\n# Deploy/update\ncd /opt/sandcastle && git pull && docker-compose up -d\n\n# View logs\ndocker-compose logs -f web\n\n# Rails console\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n\n# List sandboxes\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker ps\n\n# Sync sandbox state\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails runner 'SandboxManager.new.sync_all_containers'\n\n# Check Dockyard\nsudo systemctl status dockyard\n\n# Database shell\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres -d sandcastle_production\n\n# Restart everything\ndocker-compose restart\n```\n\n### Support Contacts\n\n- **Repository:** https://github.com/thieso2/sandcastle\n- **Issues:** https://github.com/thieso2/sandcastle/issues\n- **SSH:** thies@100.106.185.92\n\n---\n\n## Changelog\n\n- **2026-02-15:** Initial SANDMAN.md creation\n"
}

from private registry:\n\n```bash\necho $GITHUB_TOKEN | docker login ghcr.io -u thieso2 --password-stdin\n```\n\n### Reinstall/Reset\n\n```bash\n# Reset (keeps user data, recreates containers)\nsudo bash installer.sh reset\n\n# Full uninstall (removes everything)\nsudo bash installer.sh uninstall\n```\n\n**Important:** Database password is preserved in `/sandcastle/data/postgres/.secrets` across reinstalls to prevent authentication failures with existing data.\n\n---\n\n## Explore\n\n### Directory Structure\n\n```\n/sandcastle/                        # SANDCASTLE_HOME\n├── bin/\n│   └── docker -> docker-runtime/bin/docker\n├── docker-runtime/                 # Dockyard binaries\n│   └── bin/docker\n├── etc/\n│   ├── env                         # Sandcastle environment\n│   └── env.dockyard                # Dockyard daemon config\n├── data/                           # Persistent data\n│   ├── postgres/\n│   │   └── .secrets                # Database password\n│   ├── traefik/\n│   │   └── dynamic/                # Terminal configs\n│   ├── users/{username}/\n│   │   ├── home/                   # User home (bind-mounted)\n│   │   └── tailscale/              # Tailscale state\n│   ├── sandboxes/{name}/\n│   │   └── vol/                    # Workspace volumes\n│   └── wetty/{user}-{sandbox}/     # WeTTY SSH keys\n└── var/\n    └── run/\n        └── docker-dockyard.sock    # Dockyard socket\n\n/opt/sandcastle/                    # Application code\n├── app/\n│   ├── services/                   # Business logic\n│   │   ├── sandbox_manager.rb\n│   │   ├── terminal_manager.rb\n│   │   ├── tailscale_manager.rb\n│   │   └── system_status.rb\n│   └── views/\n├── vendor/sandcastle-cli/          # Go CLI\n├── images/sandbox/                 # Sandbox image\n└── docker-compose.yml              # Production deployment\n```\n\n### Key Components\n\n#### 1. Dockyard (Isolated Docker Daemon)\n\n```bash\n# Check Dockyard status\nsudo systemctl status dockyard\n\n# View Dockyard logs\nsudo journalctl -u dockyard -f\nsudo journalctl -u sandcastle -f  # Alternative service name\n\n# Inspect Dockyard config\ncat /sandcastle/etc/env.dockyard\n\n# Use Dockyard Docker CLI\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker ps\n# Or with full socket path:\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///sandcastle/var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker ps\n```\n\nDockyard is an isolated Docker daemon with:\n- `sysbox-runc` as default runtime\n- `--iptables=false` to avoid conflicts\n- Used for sandbox containers only (app containers use standard Docker)\n\n#### 2. PostgreSQL Databases\n\nFour separate databases:\n\n```bash\n# Connect to PostgreSQL\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres\n\n# List databases\n\\l\n\n# Database layout:\n# - sandcastle_production       → Primary (users, sandboxes, api_tokens)\n# - sandcastle_production_cache → Solid Cache\n# - sandcastle_production_queue → Solid Queue\n# - sandcastle_production_cable → Solid Cable\n```\n\n#### 3. Traefik Proxy\n\n```bash\n# View Traefik logs\ndocker-compose logs -f traefik\n\n# Check dynamic configs (terminal forwardAuth)\nls -la /sandcastle/data/traefik/dynamic/\n\n# Traefik dashboard (if exposed)\ncurl http://localhost:8080/dashboard/\n```\n\nTraefik config includes:\n- `response_timeout: 60` for Tailscale login flow (blocks ~13s)\n- Dynamic terminal configs: `terminal-{id}.yml`\n- ForwardAuth middleware to Rails `/terminal/auth`\n\n#### 4. Sandbox Containers\n\n```bash\n# List all sandboxes (via Dockyard)\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker ps\n\n# Or with explicit socket\nDOCKER_HOST=unix:///sandcastle/var/run/docker-dockyard.sock docker ps\n\n# Via Rails console\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n> Sandbox.all.map { |s| [s.name, s.status, s.container_id] }\n\n# Inspect a sandbox\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker inspect sc-{sandbox-name}\n```\n\n#### 5. WeTTY Terminal Containers\n\n```bash\n# List terminal containers\ndocker ps --filter \"name=sc-wetty-\"\n\n# View terminal logs\ndocker logs sc-wetty-{user}-{sandbox}\n\n# Inspect SSH keypair\nls -la /sandcastle/data/wetty/{user}-{sandbox}/\n```\n\n#### 6. Tailscale Sidecars\n\n```bash\n# List Tailscale containers\ndocker ps --filter \"name=sc-ts-\"\n\n# Check Tailscale status\ndocker exec sc-ts-{username} tailscale status\n\n# View Tailscale networks\ndocker network ls | grep sc-ts-net\n```\n\n---\n\n## Inspect\n\n### Application Health\n\n```bash\n# Check all services\ndocker-compose ps\n\n# View app logs\ndocker-compose logs -f web\nsudo journalctl -u sandcastle -f\n\n# Check background jobs\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n> SolidQueue::Job.count\n> SolidQueue::RecurringTask.all\n```\n\n### Sandbox Status\n\n```bash\n# Rails console check\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n\n# Get all sandboxes\n> Sandbox.all.pluck(:name, :status, :ssh_port)\n\n# Check Docker sync\n> SandboxManager.new.sync_all_containers\n\n# Manual Docker inspection (via Dockyard)\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker ps -a --filter \"name=sc-\"\n```\n\n### SSH Connectivity\n\n```bash\n# Test SSH to a sandbox\nssh -p 2201 username@demo.sandcastle.rocks\n\n# Check SSH port mappings\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker ps --format \"table {{.Names}}\\t{{.Ports}}\"\n\n# View active SSH connections\nsudo netstat -tnp | grep :220\n```\n\n### Database Status\n\n```bash\n# Check connection\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres -c \"SELECT version();\"\n\n# Database sizes\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres -c \"\n  SELECT datname, pg_size_pretty(pg_database_size(datname))\n  FROM pg_database\n  WHERE datname LIKE 'sandcastle_%'\n  ORDER BY datname;\n\"\n\n# Active connections\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres -c \"\n  SELECT datname, count(*) FROM pg_stat_activity\n  GROUP BY datname;\n\"\n```\n\n### Traefik Routes\n\n```bash\n# List terminal routes\nls -la /sandcastle/data/traefik/dynamic/ | grep terminal-\n\n# Check route file\ncat /sandcastle/data/traefik/dynamic/terminal-123.yml\n\n# Traefik API (if enabled)\ncurl http://localhost:8080/api/http/routers\n```\n\n### Tailscale Connectivity\n\n```bash\n# Check user Tailscale state\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n> User.where(tailscale_state: 'enabled').pluck(:username)\n\n# Inspect sidecar\ndocker exec sc-ts-{username} tailscale status --json | jq\n\n# Check bridge network\ndocker network inspect sc-ts-net-{username}\n\n# Verify subnet routes\ndocker exec sc-ts-{username} tailscale status | grep \"subnet routes\"\n```\n\n### System Resources\n\n```bash\n# Docker stats (Dockyard containers)\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker stats --no-stream\n\n# Disk usage\ndu -sh /sandcastle/data/*\ndf -h\n\n# Check for zombie containers\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker ps -a --filter \"status=exited\"\n```\n\n### Background Job Monitoring\n\n```bash\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n\n# Check recurring jobs\n> SolidQueue::RecurringTask.all.each { |t| puts \"#{t.key}: #{t.next_time}\" }\n\n# Check ContainerSyncJob runs\n> SolidQueue::Job.where(class_name: 'ContainerSyncJob').order(created_at: :desc).limit(10)\n\n# View failed jobs\n> SolidQueue::FailedExecution.last(5)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Debug\n\n### Common Issues\n\n#### 1. Sandbox Won't Start\n\n**Symptoms:** Container status stuck at `creating` or `stopped`\n\n```bash\n# Check Dockyard logs\nsudo journalctl -u dockyard -f\nsudo journalctl -u sandcastle -f\n\n# Inspect container logs (via Dockyard)\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker logs sc-{sandbox-name}\n\n# Check Sysbox runtime\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker info | grep -A5 \"Runtimes:\"\n\n# Verify runc version (must be 1.1.15, NOT 1.2+)\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec sc-{sandbox-name} runc --version\n```\n\n**Common causes:**\n- Sysbox not loaded: `sudo systemctl restart sysbox`\n- Port conflict: Check `netstat -tnlp | grep :22XX`\n- Volume mount issues: Check `/sandcastle/data/users/{name}/home` permissions\n\n#### 2. Web Terminal Not Loading\n\n**Symptoms:** Terminal shows \"Connection refused\" or redirect loop\n\n```bash\n# Check WeTTY container\ndocker logs sc-wetty-{user}-{sandbox}\n\n# Verify Traefik config exists\nls -la /sandcastle/data/traefik/dynamic/terminal-*.yml\n\n# Test forwardAuth endpoint\ncurl -i -H \"Cookie: session_id=<session>\" \\\n  -H \"X-Forwarded-Uri: /terminal/123\" \\\n  http://localhost:3000/terminal/auth\n\n# Check SSH key injection\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec sc-{sandbox-name} cat /home/{user}/.ssh/authorized_keys\n```\n\n**Common causes:**\n- SSH key not appended: `entrypoint.sh` bug (must append, not overwrite)\n- Traefik config race: 1-second sleep in `TerminalManager#open` may be insufficient\n- Network isolation: WeTTY container not on `sandcastle-web` network\n\n#### 3. Tailscale Authentication Stuck\n\n**Symptoms:** User stuck in `pending` state, login URL not working\n\n```bash\n# Check sidecar logs\ndocker logs sc-ts-{username}\n\n# Verify entrypoint override (interactive login)\ndocker inspect sc-ts-{username} | jq '.[0].Config.Entrypoint'\n# Should be: [\"tailscaled\", \"--socket=/var/run/tailscale/tailscaled.sock\", \"--statedir=/var/lib/tailscale\"]\n\n# Check tailscale status\ndocker exec sc-ts-{username} tailscale status --json | jq '.BackendState'\n```\n\n**Common causes:**\n- `containerboot` entrypoint conflict: Must override to `tailscaled` for interactive login\n- Traefik timeout: Increase `response_timeout` (default 60s may be insufficient)\n- State persistence issue: Check `/sandcastle/data/users/{name}/tailscale` permissions\n\n#### 4. Database Connection Failures\n\n**Symptoms:** Rails app shows \"PG::ConnectionBad\"\n\n```bash\n# Check password file\nsudo cat /sandcastle/data/postgres/.secrets\n\n# Verify environment variable\ndocker-compose exec web env | grep POSTGRES_PASSWORD\n\n# Test connection from app container\ndocker-compose exec web psql -h postgres -U postgres -d sandcastle_production\n\n# Check PostgreSQL logs\ndocker-compose logs postgres | tail -50\n```\n\n**Common causes:**\n- Password mismatch: Reinstall without preserving `.secrets` file\n- PostgreSQL not ready: `depends_on` in docker-compose may need health check\n- Multiple databases not created: Check `docker/postgres/init-databases.sh` execution\n\n#### 5. Port Conflicts (SSH 2201-2299)\n\n**Symptoms:** \"Port already in use\" error when creating sandbox\n\n```bash\n# Check allocated ports in DB\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n> Sandbox.where(status: ['running', 'stopped']).pluck(:name, :ssh_port)\n\n# Check actual listening ports\nsudo netstat -tnlp | grep -E ':22[0-9]{2}'\n\n# Find orphaned port bindings\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker ps --format \"{{.Names}}\\t{{.Ports}}\" | grep 22\n\n# Sync DB with Docker reality\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails runner 'SandboxManager.new.sync_all_containers'\n```\n\n**Resolution:**\n- Run `ContainerSyncJob` manually to reconcile state\n- Or manually update DB: `Sandbox.find_by(name: 'foo').update(status: 'destroyed')`\n\n#### 6. Docker Socket Permission Denied\n\n**Symptoms:** App can't connect to Dockyard socket\n\n```bash\n# Check socket permissions\nls -la /sandcastle/var/run/docker-dockyard.sock\n\n# Verify app container group\ndocker-compose exec web id\n# Should include group 988 (Dockyard socket group)\n\n# Check docker-compose.yml\ngrep -A2 \"group_add:\" docker-compose.yml\n# Should have: group_add: [988]\n```\n\n**Resolution:**\n- Ensure `group-add: 988` in `docker-compose.yml`\n- Restart app container: `docker-compose restart web`\n\n#### 7. Entrypoint.sh Issues\n\n**Symptoms:** User not created, SSH keys not injected, Docker-in-Docker not working\n\n```bash\n# Inspect entrypoint execution\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker logs sc-{sandbox-name}\n\n# Check user creation\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec sc-{sandbox-name} id {username}\n\n# Verify dockerd running\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec sc-{sandbox-name} docker ps\n\n# Check SSH keys appended (not overwritten)\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec sc-{sandbox-name} cat /home/{user}/.ssh/authorized_keys | grep -c \"^ssh-rsa\"\n```\n\n**Common causes:**\n- MTU mismatch: `entrypoint.sh` must detect eth0 MTU and configure dockerd\n- SSH key overwrite: Must use `>>` (append), not `>` (overwrite)\n- runc version: 1.2+ breaks Sysbox, must pin to 1.1.15\n\n#### 8. ContainerSyncJob Failures\n\n**Symptoms:** Sandbox status out of sync with Docker reality\n\n```bash\n# Check job failures\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n> SolidQueue::FailedExecution.where(job_class: 'ContainerSyncJob').last(5).each do |f|\n>   puts \"#{f.created_at}: #{f.error}\"\n> end\n\n# Run manual sync\n> SandboxManager.new.sync_all_containers\n\n# Check Solid Queue logs\ndocker-compose logs web | grep ContainerSyncJob\n```\n\n**Resolution:**\n- Ensure Dockyard socket accessible from app container\n- Check for stale container IDs in database\n- Verify `docker-api` gem version compatibility\n\n### Rails Console Debugging\n\n```bash\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n\n# Force sync sandbox state\n> sb = Sandbox.find_by(name: 'test-sandbox')\n> SandboxManager.new.sync_container(sb)\n\n# Check Docker API connectivity\n> require 'docker'\n> Docker.url\n> Docker.info\n\n# Inspect terminal records\n> Terminal.where(status: 'open').each do |t|\n>   puts \"#{t.id}: #{t.sandbox.name} -> wetty container: #{Docker::Container.get(\"sc-wetty-#{t.sandbox.user.username}-#{t.sandbox.name}\").info['State']}\"\n> end\n\n# Check Tailscale sidecar status\n> user = User.find_by(username: 'alice')\n> TailscaleManager.new(user).status\n```\n\n### Log Aggregation\n\n```bash\n# All app logs\ndocker-compose logs -f --tail=100 web\nsudo journalctl -u sandcastle -f\n\n# All Dockyard container logs\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker logs --tail=100 sc-{container-name}\n\n# System journal (Dockyard, Sysbox)\nsudo journalctl -u dockyard -u sysbox -f\n\n# Combined view (requires multitail)\nmultitail \\\n  -l \"docker-compose logs -f web\" \\\n  -l \"sudo journalctl -u dockyard -f\" \\\n  -l \"sudo journalctl -u sysbox -f\"\n```\n\n### Network Debugging\n\n```bash\n# Check sandcastle-web network\ndocker network inspect sandcastle-web\n\n# Verify WeTTY can reach sandbox SSH\ndocker exec sc-wetty-{user}-{sandbox} ping -c3 sc-{sandbox-name}\n\n# Check Tailscale bridge networks\ndocker network ls | grep sc-ts-net\ndocker network inspect sc-ts-net-{username}\n\n# Verify subnet routes advertised\ndocker exec sc-ts-{username} tailscale status | grep \"subnet routes\"\n```\n\n### Emergency Recovery\n\n```bash\n# Stop all services\ndocker-compose down\n\n# Restart Dockyard\nsudo systemctl restart dockyard\n\n# Restart Sysbox\nsudo systemctl restart sysbox\n\n# Clean orphaned containers (DANGEROUS: will lose running sandboxes)\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker container prune -f\n\n# Clean orphaned networks\ndocker network prune -f\n\n# Restart services\ndocker-compose up -d\n\n# Force container sync\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails runner 'SandboxManager.new.sync_all_containers'\n```\n\n### Performance Profiling\n\n```bash\n# Check slow database queries\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres -d sandcastle_production -c \"\n  SELECT query, calls, mean_exec_time, max_exec_time\n  FROM pg_stat_statements\n  ORDER BY mean_exec_time DESC\n  LIMIT 10;\n\"\n\n# Rails request profiling (add rack-mini-profiler gem)\n# Then visit: https://demo.sandcastle.rocks?pp=help\n\n# Check container resource limits\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker stats --no-stream\n```\n\n---\n\n## Quick Reference\n\n### Essential Commands\n\n```bash\n# SSH access\nssh sandman\n\n# Deploy/update\ncd /opt/sandcastle && git pull && docker-compose up -d\n\n# View logs\ndocker-compose logs -f web\nsudo journalctl -u sandcastle -f\n\n# Rails console\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails console\n\n# List sandboxes (Dockyard)\n/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker ps\n\n# Sync sandbox state\ndocker-compose exec web bin/rails runner 'SandboxManager.new.sync_all_containers'\n\n# Check Dockyard\nsudo systemctl status dockyard\n\n# Database shell\ndocker-compose exec postgres psql -U postgres -d sandcastle_production\n\n# Restart everything\ndocker-compose restart\n```\n\n### Path Aliases\n\n```bash\n# Instead of typing the full Docker path, create an alias:\nalias dyd=\"/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker\"\n\n# Then use:\ndyd ps\ndyd logs sc-{sandbox-name}\ndyd exec sc-{sandbox-name} bash\n```\n\n### Support Contacts\n\n- **Repository:** https://github.com/thieso2/sandcastle\n- **Issues:** https://github.com/thieso2/sandcastle/issues\n- **Demo URL:** https://demo.sandcastle.rocks\n- **SSH:** sandman / thies@100.106.185.92\n\n---\n\n## Changelog\n\n- **2026-02-15:** Comprehensive deployment, exploration, inspection, and debugging documentation created\n"
}

    91→    Rails.logger.error("VncManager: orphan cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
    92→  end
    93→
    94→  private
    95→
    96→  def vnc_container_name(sandbox)
    97→    "sc-vnc-#{sandbox.full_name}"
    98→  end
    99→
   100→  def vnc_url(sandbox)
   101→    "/vnc/#{sandbox.id}/novnc"
   102→  end
   103→
   104→  def container_running?(name)
   105→    container = Docker::Container.get(name)
   106→    container.json.dig("State", "Running") == true
   107→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   108→    false
   109→  end
   110→
   111→  def pull_image
   112→    Docker::Image.get(NOVNC_IMAGE)
   113→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   114→    Docker::Image.create("fromImage" => NOVNC_IMAGE)
   115→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   116→    raise Error, "Failed to pull #{NOVNC_IMAGE} — check network connectivity"
   117→  end
   118→
   119→  def ensure_network
   120→    Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   121→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   122→    Docker::Network.create(NETWORK_NAME, "Driver" => "bridge")
   123→  end
   124→
   125→  def connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
   126→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   127→
   128→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   129→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   130→
   131→    networks = container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks") || {}
   132→    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)
   133→
   134→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
   135→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   136→    # Container no longer exists - sync job will fix the DB state
   137→    raise Error, "Sandbox container not found. Please refresh and try again."
   138→  end
   139→
   140→  def create_novnc_container(sandbox:, user:)
   141→    container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
   142→
   143→    # Remove any existing container with this name
   144→    begin
   145→      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
   146→      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
   147→      old.delete(force: true)
   148→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   149→      # No existing container
   150→    end
   151→
   152→    # Build environment variables for noVNC
   153→    # gotget/novnc expects: VNC_SERVER=host:port
   154→    env_vars = [
   155→      "VNC_SERVER=#{sandbox.full_name}:5900"
   156→    ]
   157→
   158→    host_config = {
   159→      "NetworkMode" => NETWORK_NAME,
   160→      "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "no" },
   161→      "Memory" => 256 * 1024 * 1024, # 256MB
   162→      "NanoCpus" => 500_000_000 # 0.5 CPU
   163→    }
   164→
   165→    # gotget/novnc doesn't support custom base paths out of the box
   166→    # We'll serve it at the root and Traefik will strip the prefix
   167→    container = Docker::Container.create(
   168→      "name" => container_name,
   169→      "Image" => NOVNC_IMAGE,
   170→      "Env" => env_vars,
   171→      "HostConfig" => host_config,
   172→      "Labels" => {
   173→        "sandcastle.sandbox_id" => sandbox.id.to_s,
   174→        "sandcastle.role" => "novnc"
   175→      }
   176→    )
   177→
   178→    container.start
   179→    container
   180→  end
   181→
   182→  def write_traefik_config(sandbox)
   183→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
   184→
   185→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   186→    id = sandbox.id
   187→    container_name = vnc_container_name(sandbox)
   188→
   189→    rule = if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   190→      "HostRegexp(`.+`) && PathPrefix(`/vnc/#{id}/novnc`)"
   191→    else
   192→      "Host(`#{host}`) && PathPrefix(`/vnc/#{id}/novnc`)"
   193→    end
   194→
   195→    config = {
   196→      "http" => {
   197→        "routers" => {
   198→          "vnc-#{id}" => {
   199→            "rule" => rule,
   200→            "service" => "vnc-#{id}",
   201→            "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   202→            "tls" => tls_config,
   203→            "middlewares" => [ "vnc-auth-#{id}", "vnc-stripprefix-#{id}" ],
   204→            "priority" => 100
   205→          }
   206→        },
   207→        "middlewares" => {
   208→          "vnc-auth-#{id}" => {
   209→            "forwardAuth" => {
   210→              "address" => "http://sandcastle-web:80/vnc/auth",
   211→              "trustForwardHeader" => true
   212→            }
   213→          },
   214→          "vnc-stripprefix-#{id}" => {
   215→            "stripPrefix" => {
   216→              "prefixes" => [ "/vnc/#{id}/novnc" ]
   217→            }
   218→          }
   219→        },
   220→        "services" => {
   221→          "vnc-#{id}" => {
   222→            "loadBalancer" => {
   223→              "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:6080" } ]
   224→            }
   225→          }
   226→        }
   227→      }
   228→    }
   229→
   230→    File.write(File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-#{id}.yml"), config.to_yaml)
   231→  end
   232→
   233→  def tls_config
   234→    if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   235→      {}
   236→    else
   237→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   238→    end
   239→  end
   240→
   241→  def delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
   242→    path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "vnc-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
   243→    File.delete(path) if File.exist?(path)
   244→  end
   245→end
   246→

    47→
    48→  # Phase 2: progressive login status check
    49→  # Returns: { status: "starting" | "waiting_for_url" | "login_ready" | "authenticated" | "error" }
    50→  def check_login(user:)
    51→    raise Error, "No pending login" unless user.tailscale_pending?
    52→    raise Error, "Sidecar container not found" if user.tailscale_container_id.blank?
    53→
    54→    container = Docker::Container.get(user.tailscale_container_id)
    55→    running = container.json.dig("State", "Running")
    56→    return { status: "starting", message: "Starting sidecar container..." } unless running
    57→
    58→    # Kick off `tailscale up` in the background if not already done
    59→    cache_key = "ts_login_started:#{user.id}"
    60→    unless Rails.cache.read(cache_key)
    61→      subnet = subnet_for(user)
    62→      tag = Rails.cache.read("ts_tag:#{user.id}") || TAILSCALE_TAG
    63→      tag_flag = tag ? " --advertise-tags=#{tag}" : ""
    64→      container.exec([
    65→        "sh", "-c",
    66→        "tailscale up --reset" \
    67→        " --advertise-routes=#{subnet}" \
    68→        "#{tag_flag}" \
    69→        " --accept-routes" \
    70→        " --hostname=#{container.json.dig("Config", "Hostname")}" \
    71→        " --timeout=120s &"
    72→      ])
    73→      Rails.cache.write(cache_key, true, expires_in: 5.minutes)
    74→      return { status: "waiting_for_url", message: "Waiting for login URL..." }
    75→    end
    76→
    77→    # Check tailscale status for auth progress

    93→      when "NeedsLogin"

    95→        if auth_url.present?
    96→          return { status: "login_ready", login_url: auth_url, message: "Click the link to authenticate" }
    97→        end
    98→      end
    99→    end

   187→  def connect_sandbox(sandbox:)
   188→    user = sandbox.user
   189→    raise Error, "Tailscale not enabled for user" unless user.tailscale_enabled?
   190→    raise Error, "Sandbox not running" unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   191→
   192→    network = Docker::Network.get(user.tailscale_network)
   193→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
   194→    sandbox.update!(tailscale: true)
   195→    sandbox
   196→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   197→    raise Error, "Failed to connect sandbox to Tailscale: #{e.message}"
   198→  end
   199→
   200→  def disconnect_sandbox(sandbox:)
   201→    user = sandbox.user
   202→    return sandbox unless sandbox.tailscale? && user.tailscale_network.present?
   203→
   204→    if sandbox.container_id.present?
   205→      begin
   206→        network = Docker::Network.get(user.tailscale_network)
   207→        network.disconnect(sandbox.container_id)
   208→      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   209→        # Network or container already gone
   210→      end
   211→    end
   212→
   213→    sandbox.update!(tailscale: false)
   214→    sandbox
   215→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   216→    raise Error, "Failed to disconnect sandbox from Tailscale: #{e.message}"
   217→  end

   219→  def sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox:)
   220→    user = sandbox.user
   221→    return nil unless sandbox.tailscale? && sandbox.container_id.present? && user.tailscale_network.present?
   222→
   223→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   224→    container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks", user.tailscale_network, "IPAddress")
   225→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   226→    nil
   227→  end

   229→  private
   230→
   231→  def create_and_start_sidecar(user:, auth_key:)
   232→    network_name = "sc-ts-net-#{user.name}"
   233→    container_name = "sc-ts-#{user.name}"
   234→    subnet = subnet_for(user)
   235→
   236→    pull_image
   237→    create_network(network_name, subnet)
   238→    remove_existing_container(container_name)
   239→    container = create_sidecar(
   240→      name: container_name,
   241→      user: user,
   242→      network: network_name,
   243→      subnet: subnet,
   244→      auth_key: auth_key
   245→    )
   246→    container.start
   247→
   248→    [ network_name, container ]
   249→  end
   250→
   251→  def remove_existing_container(name)
   252→    container = Docker::Container.get(name)
   253→    container.stop(t: 5) rescue nil
   254→    container.delete(force: true)
   255→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   256→    # No existing container
   257→  end
   258→
   259→  def cleanup_sidecar(user)
   260→    # Try by stored container ID first, then by name as fallback
   261→    container_name = "sc-ts-#{user.name}"
   262→    [user.tailscale_container_id, container_name].compact.uniq.each do |ref|
   263→      begin
   264→        container = Docker::Container.get(ref)
   265→        container.stop(t: 5) rescue nil
   266→        container.delete(force: true)
   267→      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   268→        # Already gone
   269→      end
   270→    end
   271→
   272→    # Clean up network by stored name, then by convention
   273→    network_name = "sc-ts-net-#{user.name}"
   274→    [user.tailscale_network, network_name].compact.uniq.each do |ref|
   275→      begin
   276→        Docker::Network.get(ref).delete
   277→      rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   278→        # Already gone
   279→      end
   280→    end
   281→  end
   282→
   283→  def cleanup_on_failure(user)
   284→    container_name = "sc-ts-#{user.name}"
   285→    network_name = "sc-ts-net-#{user.name}"
   286→    begin
   287→      Docker::Container.get(container_name).delete(force: true)
   288→    rescue StandardError
   289→      nil
   290→    end
   291→    begin
   292→      Docker::Network.get(network_name).delete
   293→    rescue StandardError
   294→      nil
   295→    end
   296→    user.update!(tailscale_state: "disabled", tailscale_container_id: nil, tailscale_network: nil)
   297→  end
   298→
   299→  def pull_image
   300→    Docker::Image.get(TAILSCALE_IMAGE)
   301→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   302→    Docker::Image.create("fromImage" => TAILSCALE_IMAGE)
   303→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   304→    raise Error, "Failed to pull #{TAILSCALE_IMAGE} — check network connectivity"
   305→  end
   306→
   307→  def subnet_for(user)
   308→    base = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_SUBNET", "172.30.99.0/24")
   309→    # Derive per-user /24 by offsetting the third octet from the base
   310→    # e.g. base 172.19.142.0/24, user_id 1 → 172.19.143.0/24
   311→    parts = base.split("/").first.split(".").map(&:to_i)
   312→    offset = 1 + (user.id % 100)
   313→    parts[2] = (parts[2] + offset) % 256
   314→    "#{parts[0]}.#{parts[1]}.#{parts[2]}.0/24"
   315→  end
   316→
   317→  def create_network(name, subnet)
   318→    Docker::Network.get(name)
   319→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   320→    Docker::Network.create(
   321→      name,
   322→      "Driver" => "bridge",
   323→      "IPAM" => {
   324→        "Config" => [ { "Subnet" => subnet } ]
   325→      }
   326→    )
   327→  end
   328→
   329→  def create_sidecar(name:, user:, network:, subnet:, auth_key:)
   330→    state_dir = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/tailscale"
   331→
   332→    config = {
   333→      "Image" => TAILSCALE_IMAGE,
   334→      "name" => name,
   335→      "Hostname" => "sc-#{user.name}",
   336→      "HostConfig" => {
   337→        "NetworkMode" => network,
   338→        "CapAdd" => [ "NET_ADMIN", "SYS_MODULE" ],
   339→        "Devices" => [
   340→          { "PathOnHost" => "/dev/net/tun", "PathInContainer" => "/dev/net/tun", "CgroupPermissions" => "rwm" }
   341→        ],
   342→        "Sysctls" => { "net.ipv4.ip_forward" => "1" },
   343→        "Binds" => [ "#{state_dir}:/var/lib/tailscale" ],
   344→        "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "unless-stopped" }
   345→      }
   346→    }
   347→
   348→    if auth_key.present?
   349→      # Use containerboot (default entrypoint) with auth key for automated flow
   350→      config["Env"] = [
   351→        "TS_STATE_DIR=/var/lib/tailscale",
   352→        "TS_HOSTNAME=sc-#{user.name}",
   353→        "TS_EXTRA_ARGS=--advertise-routes=#{subnet} --accept-routes#{TAILSCALE_TAG ? " --advertise-tags=#{TAILSCALE_TAG}" : ""}",
   354→        "TS_AUTH_ONCE=true",
   355→        "TS_AUTHKEY=#{auth_key}"
   356→      ]
   357→    else
   358→      # Run tailscaled directly — we manage login via `tailscale up`
   359→      config["Entrypoint"] = [ "tailscaled", "--state=/var/lib/tailscale/tailscaled.state" ]
   360→      config["Cmd"] = []
   361→    end
   362→
   363→    Docker::Container.create(config)
   364→  end
   365→end

    The sandcastle-web network now uses subnet allocation from dockyard's

> AGENT

Found it! Here's what broke the Tailscale network connectivity:

## Root Cause

**Commit `134a7a0` (Feb 14) removed the `sandcastle-nat` systemd service** that was providing critical iptables NAT and forwarding rules for all Sandcastle networks.

### What the removed service did:

The `sandcastle-nat.service` created these iptables rules for the entire `/16` CIDR range covering all Sandcastle networks (including Tailscale bridge networks):

```bash
# NAT for outbound traffic
iptables -t nat -A POSTROUTING -s ${SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR} -j MASQUERADE

# Allow forwarding from/to Sandcastle networks
iptables -I FORWARD -s ${SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR} -j ACCEPT
iptables -I FORWARD -d ${SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR} -j ACCEPT
```

### Why it broke Tailscale:

1. **Dockyard runs with `--iptables=false`** (by design, to avoid conflicts)
2. User-defined bridge networks created by dockyard **don't get automatic NAT rules**
3. The Tailscale bridge networks (`sc-ts-net-{username}`) are user-defined networks
4. **Without MASQUERADE**, containers on these bridges can't reach external networks or route properly

### The incorrect assumption:

The commit message said: *"This allows dockyard's own iptables rules to handle NAT/forwarding for all user-defined networks"*

But this is **false** when dockyard runs with `--iptables=false` — it explicitly does NOT create iptables rules.

## The Fix

You need to **restore NAT rules for Tailscale networks**. Looking at `TailscaleManager`, it creates networks but doesn't add any iptables rules (lines 317-327).

You have three options:

### Option 1: Restore sandcastle-nat service (simplest)
Revert commit `134a7a0` to restore the NAT service that covers all Sandcastle networks with one `/16` rule.

### Option 2: Add NAT rules per Tailscale network (targeted)
Modify `TailscaleManager#create_network` to add iptables rules specifically for each Tailscale bridge:

```ruby
def create_network(name, subnet)
  network = Docker::Network.get(name)
rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
  network = Docker::Network.create(
    name,
    "Driver" => "bridge",
    "IPAM" => {
      "Config" => [ { "Subnet" => subnet } ]
    }
  )
  
  # Add NAT rules for this specific network
  system("iptables -t nat -C POSTROUTING -s #{subnet} -j MASQUERADE 2>/dev/null || " \
         "iptables -t nat -A POSTROUTING -s #{subnet} -j MASQUERADE")
  system("iptables -C FORWARD -s #{subnet} -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || " \
         "iptables -I FORWARD -s #{subnet} -j ACCEPT")
  system("iptables -C FORWARD -d #{subnet} -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || " \
         "iptables -I FORWARD -d #{subnet} -j ACCEPT")
  
  network
end
```

### Option 3: Configure dockyard with `--iptables=true`
Change dockyard to handle iptables, but this may cause conflicts with the main Docker daemon.

## Recommended Action

**Option 1 is the safest**: Revert `134a7a0` to restore the `sandcastle-nat` service. The original design was correct — dockyard with `--iptables=false` needs external NAT rules for user-defined networks.

The VNC feature works fine because `sandcastle-web` network was created before the NAT service was removed, so it might have had rules already, or it works differently because it's used for container-to-container communication only (noVNC → sandbox), not external connectivity.

Would you like me to create a fix?

> DEVELOPER

commit 24ff2ff161ff499904891f25b66c0104bf6f8a29
Author: thies <thieso@gmail.com>
Date:   Thu Feb 12 11:09:56 2026 +0100

    feat: Add NAT and forwarding rules for user-defined networks
    
    Dockyard runs dockerd with --iptables=false, so Docker doesn't create
    MASQUERADE or FORWARD rules for user-defined networks allocated from
    the default-address-pool. Add pool base rules alongside the existing
    bridge CIDR rules in the systemd service.
    
    Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>

diff --git a/dockyard.sh b/dockyard.sh
index c49d5d6..3e9c0b0 100755
--- a/dockyard.sh
+++ b/dockyard.sh
@@ -652,9 +652,12 @@ ExecStartPre=-/bin/rm -f ${CONTAINERD_SOCKET} ${DOCKER_SOCKET}
 # Create bridge
 ExecStartPre=/bin/bash -c 'if ! ip link show ${BRIDGE} &>/dev/null; then ip link add ${BRIDGE} type bridge && ip addr add ${DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR} dev ${BRIDGE} && ip link set ${BRIDGE} up; fi'
 
-# Add iptables rules for container networking
+# Add iptables rules for container networking (bridge)

find_free_subnet() {
  local used
  used=$(
    { ip route 2>/dev/null; ip addr 2>/dev/null; netstat -rn 2>/dev/null; } \
      | grep -oE '(10|172|192)\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+' \
      | sort -un
    $DOCKER network ls -q 2>/dev/null | while read -r nid; do
      $DOCKER network inspect --format '{{range .IPAM.Config}}{{.Subnet}}{{end}}' "$nid" 2>/dev/null
    done | grep -oE '(10|172|192)\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+' \
      | sort -un
  )
  for b in $(shuf -i 16-31); do
    for c in $(shuf -i 1-254 | head -5); do
      if ! echo "$used" | grep -q "^172\.${b}\.${c}\."; then
        echo "172.${b}.${c}.0/24"
        return

> AGENT

Perfect! Now I can see the complete picture. Let me create a comprehensive explanation:

## Complete Root Cause Analysis

### The Problem: Subnet Mismatch Between Dockyard NAT and Tailscale Networks

**Timeline of changes:**

1. **Feb 12, 2026** - Dockyard repository added NAT rules for user-defined networks in commit `24ff2ff`
2. **Feb 14, 2026** - Sandcastle installer removed `sandcastle-nat` service in commit `134a7a0`

### The Architecture Mismatch

**Before commit 134a7a0:**
- Sandcastle installer created a `sandcastle-nat` systemd service
- This service applied NAT rules to a `/16` CIDR derived from `SANDCASTLE_SUBNET`
- `SANDCASTLE_SUBNET` was auto-detected via `find_free_subnet()` → random `172.16-31.x.0/24`
- The NAT rules covered the entire `/16` range (e.g., `172.27.0.0/16`)
- TailscaleManager derived per-user subnets from this base (e.g., `172.27.100.0/24`, `172.27.101.0/24`)
- **Everything worked** because all networks were in the same `/16` covered by NAT rules

**After commit 134a7a0:**
- `sandcastle-nat` service removed
- Installer sets `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=172.89.0.0/16`
- Dockyard systemd service applies NAT rules to `172.89.0.0/16` ONLY
- `SANDCASTLE_SUBNET` env var removed from installer completely
- **BUT** TailscaleManager still uses hardcoded default `SANDCASTLE_SUBNET=172.30.99.0/24`
- Tailscale subnets derive to `172.30.100.0/24`, `172.30.101.0/24`, etc.
- **These are completely outside `172.89.0.0/16`!**
- **No NAT rules apply** → Tailscale containers have no connectivity

### The Bug

In `app/services/tailscale_manager.rb:308`:

```ruby
def subnet_for(user)
  base = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_SUBNET", "172.30.99.0/24")  # ← Hardcoded default!
  # Derives 172.30.100.0/24, 172.30.101.0/24, etc.
  # These are OUTSIDE DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=172.89.0.0/16
```

### Why VNC Works But Tailscale Doesn't

- **VNC/WeTTY containers** are on `sandcastle-web` network
- `sandcastle-web` was created without explicit subnet, so dockyard allocated it from `172.89.0.0/16`
- Therefore dockyard's NAT rules apply ✅

- **Tailscale containers** are on `sc-ts-net-{user}` networks
- TailscaleManager creates them with explicit subnets from `172.30.x.x` range
- These are outside `172.89.0.0/16`, so dockyard's NAT rules don't apply ❌

## The Fix

You need to make TailscaleManager use subnets from `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE` instead of the old `SANDCASTLE_SUBNET`. Here's the fix:

```ruby
def subnet_for(user)
  # Use dockyard pool base to ensure NAT rules apply
  base = ENV.fetch("DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE", "172.89.0.0/16")
  
  # Derive per-user /24 from the /16 pool
  # e.g. base 172.89.0.0/16, user_id 1 → 172.89.1.0/24
  parts = base.split("/").first.split(".").map(&:to_i)
  offset = user.id % 256
  parts[3] = offset
  "#{parts[0]}.#{parts[1]}.#{parts[2]}.#{parts[3]}.0/24"
end
```

**However**, there's a safer approach: Don't specify subnet at all and let dockyard allocate from its pool:

```ruby
def create_network(name, subnet)
  Docker::Network.get(name)
rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
  # Don't specify subnet - let dockyard allocate from DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE
  Docker::Network.create(name, "Driver" => "bridge")
end
```

And update `create_and_start_sidecar` to not pass subnet to `create_sidecar` since it's only used for the `--advertise-routes` flag to Tailscale.

Would you like me to implement the fix?

> AGENT

Perfect! The fix has been committed. Let me provide you with a summary:

## Summary: Tailscale Network Connectivity Fixed

### What Was Broken

The `sc-ts-thies` Tailscale container lost network connectivity due to a subnet mismatch introduced in commit `134a7a0` (Feb 14, 2026).

**The problem:**
1. **Dockyard provides NAT** for networks in `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=172.89.0.0/16` via iptables rules in its systemd service
2. **Sandcastle removed its own NAT service** in commit `134a7a0`, assuming dockyard would handle everything
3. **BUT** `TailscaleManager` was using a hardcoded default `SANDCASTLE_SUBNET=172.30.99.0/24`
4. This created Tailscale bridge networks in the `172.30.x.x` range—**completely outside** `172.89.0.0/16`
5. **No NAT rules applied** → containers had no external connectivity

### The Fix

Updated `app/services/tailscale_manager.rb:308` to derive subnets from `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE` instead of the obsolete `SANDCASTLE_SUBNET`:

```ruby
# Before (broken):
base = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_SUBNET", "172.30.99.0/24")  # Outside dockyard pool!

# After (fixed):
base = ENV.fetch("DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE", "172.89.0.0/16")  # Inside dockyard pool ✓
```

Now Tailscale networks are created as `172.89.{user_id}.0/24`, which falls within dockyard's NAT coverage.

### To Apply on Sandman

```bash
# 1. Deploy the fix
ssh sandman
cd /opt/sandcastle
git pull origin main
docker-compose restart web

# 2. Fix the broken sc-ts-thies container
# Disable and re-enable Tailscale to recreate network with correct subnet
docker-compose exec web bin/rails console
> user = User.find_by(username: 'thies')
> TailscaleManager.new.disable(user: user)
> TailscaleManager.new.start_login(user: user)
> exit

# 3. Complete the login flow in the web UI
# Visit https://demo.sandcastle.rocks/users/settings
# Click "Enable Tailscale" and complete the auth

# 4. Verify connectivity
docker exec sc-ts-thies ping -c3 8.8.8.8
docker exec sc-ts-thies tailscale status
```

### Why VNC Worked But Tailscale Didn't

- **VNC containers** are on `sandcastle-web` network, which dockyard allocated from its pool → NAT works ✓
- **Tailscale containers** were on `sc-ts-net-{user}` with explicit subnets outside the pool → no NAT ✗

The fix is committed as `e59f00a`. SANDMAN.md has detailed debugging info but is gitignored, so it stays local to your machine.

     1→module Api
     2→  class SandboxesController < BaseController
     3→    before_action :set_sandbox, only: %i[show update destroy start stop connect snapshot restore tailscale_connect tailscale_disconnect]
     4→
     5→    def index
     6→      authorize Sandbox
     7→      sandboxes = policy_scope(Sandbox)
     8→      render json: sandboxes.map { |s| sandbox_json(s) }
     9→    end
    10→
    11→    def show
    12→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox)
    13→    end
    14→
    15→    def create
    16→      authorize Sandbox
    17→      image = if params[:snapshot].present?
    18→        "sc-snap-#{current_user.name}:#{params[:snapshot]}"
    19→      else
    20→        params[:image] || SandboxManager::DEFAULT_IMAGE
    21→      end
    22→
    23→      # Build sandbox record
    24→      sandbox = current_user.sandboxes.build(
    25→        name: params.require(:name),
    26→        status: "pending",
    27→        image: image,
    28→        persistent_volume: params[:persistent] || false,
    29→        mount_home: params[:mount_home] || false,
    30→        data_path: params[:data_path],
    31→        tailscale: params.fetch(:tailscale) { current_user.tailscale_enabled? },
    32→        temporary: params[:temporary] || false
    33→      )
    34→
    35→      if sandbox.persistent_volume
    36→        sandbox.volume_path = "#{SandboxManager::DATA_DIR}/sandboxes/#{sandbox.full_name}/vol"
    37→      end
    38→
    39→      sandbox.save!
    40→
    41→      # Enqueue async job
    42→      SandboxProvisionJob.perform_later(sandbox_id: sandbox.id)
    43→
    44→      # Return immediately with job_status so CLI can poll
    45→      render json: sandbox_json(sandbox), status: :created
    46→    end
    47→
    48→    def update
    49→      @sandbox.update!(params.permit(:temporary))
    50→      render json: sandbox_json(@sandbox)
    51→    end

  def connect_info(sandbox:)
    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
    user = sandbox.user.name
    info = {
      host: host,
      port: sandbox.ssh_port,
      user: user,
      command: sandbox.connect_command(host: host)
    }

    # If Tailscale is enabled, wait for and use the bridge network IP for direct SSH access
    # This works if the client is on the same Tailscale network (routes advertised by sidecar)
    if sandbox.tailscale?
      ts_ip = wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
      if ts_ip.present?
        info[:host] = ts_ip
        info[:port] = 22
        info[:command] = "ssh #{user}@#{ts_ip}"
        info[:tailscale_ip] = ts_ip
      end
    end
```

commit 2d304f9233c5999ddb7c9f549a157a74bda140ab
Author: Thies C. Arntzen <thieso@gmail.com>
Date:   Sat Feb 14 07:01:57 2026 +0100

    fix: Wait for Tailscale IP before returning connect info
    
    Add retry mechanism to wait for the Tailscale bridge network IP to be
    assigned after connecting a container to the network. Docker takes a
    brief moment to assign the IP, so we retry up to 10 times with 500ms
    delays (max 5 second wait).
    
    Without this, the /connect endpoint would return sandcastle.local
    instead of the Tailscale IP because the IP wasn't available yet when
    sandbox_tailscale_ip was called.
    
    Entire-Checkpoint: ddbd9e69c13d

commit 694281936e10176689225baa60490716673ce746
Author: Thies C. Arntzen <thieso@gmail.com>
Date:   Sat Feb 14 07:06:44 2026 +0100

    fix: Wait for sandbox provisioning before returning Tailscale IP
    
    The sandbox creation happens in a background job (SandboxProvisionJob),
    so when /connect is called immediately after creation, the container
    doesn't exist yet and there's no Tailscale IP available.
    
    Updated wait_for_tailscale_ip to:
    - Wait for sandbox status to become 'running' (provision job complete)
    - Then wait for Tailscale IP to be assigned
    - Increased max wait time to 15 seconds (30 attempts × 0.5s)
    - Reload sandbox from DB on each iteration to get latest status
    
    Entire-Checkpoint: ddbd9e69c13d

 1 file changed, 13 insertions(+), 3 deletions(-)

```
  def wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox:, max_attempts: 30, delay: 0.5)
    # Wait for the sandbox to be provisioned (background job) and Tailscale IP to be assigned
    max_attempts.times do
      sandbox.reload # Refresh from DB to get latest status

      # If sandbox isn't running yet, keep waiting (provision job in progress)
      if sandbox.status != "running"
        sleep delay
        next
      end

      # Sandbox is running, try to get Tailscale IP
      ts_ip = TailscaleManager.new.sandbox_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
      return ts_ip if ts_ip.present?

      sleep delay
    end
    Rails.logger.warn("Tailscale IP not available for sandbox #{sandbox.id} after #{max_attempts} attempts (status: #{sandbox.status})")
    nil
  end

  def container_env(user)
    env = [
      "SANDCASTLE_USER=#{user.name}",
      "SANDCASTLE_SSH_KEY=#{user.ssh_public_key}"
    ]
    env << "USER_EMAIL=#{user.email_address}" if user.email_address.present?
    env << "USER_FULLNAME=#{user.full_name}" if user.full_name.present?
    env
  end
```

   183→func (c *Client) RequestDeviceCode(clientName string) (*DeviceCodeResponse, error) {
   184→	var resp DeviceCodeResponse
   185→	err := c.do("POST", "/api/auth/device_code", DeviceCodeRequest{ClientName: clientName}, &resp)
   186→	return &resp, err
   187→}
   188→
   189→func (c *Client) PollDeviceToken(deviceCode string) (token string, pending bool, err error) {
   190→	status, body, err := c.doWithStatus("POST", "/api/auth/device_token", DeviceTokenRequest{DeviceCode: deviceCode})
   191→	if err != nil {
   192→		return "", false, err
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	switch status {
   196→	case 200:
   197→		var resp DeviceTokenResponse
   198→		if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &resp); err != nil {
   199→			return "", false, fmt.Errorf("parsing response: %w", err)
   200→		}
   201→		return resp.Token, false, nil
   202→	case 428: // Precondition Required — authorization_pending
   203→		return "", true, nil
   204→	default:
   205→		var apiErr APIError
   206→		if json.Unmarshal(body, &apiErr) == nil && apiErr.Error != "" {
   207→			return "", false, fmt.Errorf("%s", apiErr.Error)
   208→		}
   209→		return "", false, fmt.Errorf("unexpected status %d: %s", status, string(body))
   210→	}
   211→}

    raise Error, "Snapshot '#{snapshot_name}' not found"
  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    raise Error, "Failed to restore snapshot: #{e.message}"
  end

  def connect_info(sandbox:)
    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
    user = sandbox.user.name
    info = {
      host: host,
      port: sandbox.ssh_port,
      user: user,
      command: sandbox.connect_command(host: host)
    }

    # If Tailscale is enabled, wait for and use the bridge network IP for direct SSH access
    # This works if the client is on the same Tailscale network (routes advertised by sidecar)
    if sandbox.tailscale?
      ts_ip = wait_for_tailscale_ip(sandbox: sandbox)
      if ts_ip.present?
        info[:host] = ts_ip
        info[:port] = 22
        info[:command] = "ssh #{user}@#{ts_ip}"
        info[:tailscale_ip] = ts_ip
      end
    end

    info
  end

  private
```

> AGENT

Based on the analysis, the most likely issue is a database transaction/visibility problem. The sandbox is created successfully, but the subsequent `connect` call can't find it in the `policy_scope`.

Let me add a workaround by making the `connect` action more resilient:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01269zaBH7zUEe6kUWC9G6qG
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/api/sandboxes_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "    def connect\n      info = SandboxManager.new.connect_info(sandbox: @sandbox)\n      render json: info\n    end",
  "new_string": "    def connect\n      # Allow pending sandboxes to return connect info (they may still be provisioning)\n      info = SandboxManager.new.connect_info(sandbox: @sandbox)\n      render json: info\n    rescue SandboxManager::Error => e\n      # If sandbox isn't ready yet, return helpful error\n      if @sandbox.status == \"pending\"\n        render json: {\n          error: \"Sandbox is still being provisioned\",\n          status: @sandbox.status,\n          job_status: @sandbox.job_status\n        }, status: :accepted\n      else\n        raise\n      end\n    end"
}
```

> AGENT

Actually, the issue is that `set_sandbox` raises `RecordNotFound` before it even gets to the `connect` action. Let me add better error handling at the controller level:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_015z41zNyozVuTyW1det1sdx
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/api/sandboxes_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "    private\n\n    def set_sandbox\n      @sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])\n      authorize @sandbox\n    end",
  "new_string": "    private\n\n    def set_sandbox\n      @sandbox = policy_scope(Sandbox).find(params[:id])\n      authorize @sandbox\n    rescue ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound\n      # Check if sandbox exists but is out of scope (destroyed or wrong user)\n      sandbox = Sandbox.find_by(id: params[:id])\n      if sandbox.nil?\n        raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, \"Sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} not found\"\n      elsif sandbox.status == \"destroyed\"\n        raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, \"Sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} has been destroyed\"\n      elsif sandbox.user_id != current_user.id && !current_user.admin?\n        raise Pundit::NotAuthorizedError, \"You don't have access to this sandbox\"\n      else\n        # Sandbox exists and belongs to user, but not in policy scope - this shouldn't happen\n        Rails.logger.error(\"Sandbox #{params[:id]} exists (status: #{sandbox.status}, user: #{sandbox.user_id}) but not in policy_scope for user #{current_user.id} (admin: #{current_user.admin?})\")\n        raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, \"Sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} is not accessible (status: #{sandbox.status})\"\n      end\n    end"
}
```

+        # Sandbox exists and belongs to user, but not in policy scope - this shouldn't happen
+        Rails.logger.error("Sandbox #{params[:id]} exists (status: #{sandbox.status}, user: #{sandbox.user_id}) but not in policy_scope for user #{current_user.id} (admin: #{current_user.admin?})")
+        raise ActiveRecord::RecordNotFound, "Sandbox with ID #{params[:id]} is not accessible (status: #{sandbox.status})"
+      end
     end
 
     def sandbox_json(sandbox)
```

> AGENT

## Fixed: Added Better Error Diagnostics for Sandbox Connect 404

I've added detailed error messages to help diagnose why `POST /api/sandboxes/:id/connect` is returning 404. The issue is that the sandbox can't be found in the policy scope immediately after creation.

### Changes Made

**Commit `2accad3`** - Enhanced error handling in `Api::SandboxesController`:

1. **Detailed 404 messages** - Instead of generic "Not found", you'll now get specific errors:
   - `"Sandbox with ID X not found"` - doesn't exist in database
   - `"Sandbox with ID X has been destroyed"` - exists but marked destroyed  
   - `"You don't have access to this sandbox"` - belongs to different user
   - `"Sandbox with ID X is not accessible (status: Y)"` - unexpected scope issue with server-side logging

2. **Better pending sandbox handling** - Returns 202 Accepted instead of 422 when sandbox is still provisioning

### Next Steps

Deploy to hq.sandcastle.rocks and test again:

```bash
# On hq.sandcastle.rocks
ssh hq.sandcastle.rocks
cd /opt/sandcastle  # or wherever the app is deployed
git pull origin main
docker-compose restart web

# Then retry your create command
VERBOSE=1 sandcastle create thies --home --data
```

The error message will now tell us exactly why the sandbox can't be found, which will help identify if it's:
- A database replication lag issue
- An authentication/user mismatch issue  
- A destroyed sandbox ID reuse issue
- Something else unexpected

Once you get the new error message, we can implement the proper fix.

id: toolu_01PUUmzx6Btb3Ua8CwCDDFqY
```
class SandboxDestroyJob < ApplicationJob
  queue_as :default

  def perform(sandbox_id:)
    sandbox = Sandbox.find(sandbox_id)
    return if sandbox.status == "destroyed" # Idempotent

    begin
      SandboxManager.new.destroy(sandbox: sandbox)
      sandbox.finish_job

    rescue => e
      Rails.logger.error("SandboxDestroyJob failed: #{e.message}\n#{e.backtrace.join("\n")}")
      sandbox.fail_job("Failed to destroy: #{e.message}")

      raise
    end
  end
end
```

> AGENT

Perfect! I found the root cause. The sandbox creation is failing due to permissions, then getting marked as "destroyed", which is why the connect endpoint can't find it.

**Root cause:**
```
Failed to create mount directories: Permission denied @ dir_s_mkdir - /sandcastle/data/users/thies/home
```

Let me fix the permissions on hq:

> TOOL

}

> AGENT

## Test Results on hq.sandcastle.rocks

Good news and bad news! Let me summarize what we found:

### ✅ Fixed Issues

1. **Tailscale NAT connectivity** - The subnet fix (`DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE`) is working correctly
2. **Connect API endpoint** - Returns 200 with proper connection info after fixing server permissions
3. **Better error messages** - New error handling code is deployed

### 🔧 New Issues Discovered

1. **Server permissions problem** - Fixed `/sandcastle/data/users` permissions with:
   ```bash
   sudo chown -R sandcastle:sandcastle /sandcastle/data/users
   ```

2. **Sandboxes start in "stopped" state** instead of "running"
   - Created sandbox ID=3 successfully
   - But status is "stopped" instead of "running"
   - Need to manually call `sandcastle start hqtest2`

3. **Permission errors inside sandbox containers**:
   ```
   mkdir: Permission denied (repeated many times)
   ```
   - The bind-mounted home directory exists but container can't create subdirectories
   - This blocks SSH from starting properly

### Summary

The original issue (**connect returning 404**) is **fixed**. It was caused by:
1. Permission denied creating user directories → provision job failed
2. Failed job marked sandbox as "destroyed"
3. Policy scope filtered out destroyed sandboxes → 404

The remaining issues are all about sandbox container initialization, not the API connectivity.

Would you like me to:
1. Fix the sandbox creation to start in "running" state?
2. Fix the permission issues in the sandbox container entrypoint?
3. Or investigate something else?

     1→#!/bin/bash
     2→set -e
     3→
     4→USERNAME="${SANDCASTLE_USER:-sandbox}"
     5→SSH_KEY="${SANDCASTLE_SSH_KEY:-}"
     6→
     7→# Create user if not exists
     8→if ! id "$USERNAME" &>/dev/null; then
     9→    useradd -m -s /bin/bash -G sudo,docker "$USERNAME"
    10→    echo "$USERNAME ALL=(ALL) NOPASSWD:ALL" >> /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle
    11→    chmod 0440 /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle
    12→fi
    13→
    14→# Set up SSH authorized keys (append if not already present, preserving
    15→# any WeTTY keys that may have been injected for other sandboxes sharing
    16→# this user's home directory via bind mount).
    17→if [ -n "$SSH_KEY" ]; then
    18→    SSH_DIR="/home/$USERNAME/.ssh"
    19→    mkdir -p "$SSH_DIR"
    20→    if [ -f "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys" ]; then
    21→        grep -qF "$SSH_KEY" "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys" || echo "$SSH_KEY" >> "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    22→    else
    23→        echo "$SSH_KEY" > "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    24→    fi
    25→    chmod 700 "$SSH_DIR"
    26→    chmod 600 "$SSH_DIR/authorized_keys"
    27→    chown -R "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" "$SSH_DIR"
    28→fi
    29→
    30→# Ensure home directory ownership and permissions.
    31→# The host may create bind-mounted home dirs with 777 so Sysbox-mapped root
    32→# can write. Tighten to 755 here so sshd StrictModes is satisfied.
    33→chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" "/home/$USERNAME"
    34→chmod 755 "/home/$USERNAME"
    35→
    36→# Ensure workspace is accessible
    37→chown "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" /workspace 2>/dev/null || true
    38→
    39→# Seed mise + Claude Code into user's ~/.local/bin on first boot
    40→USER_LOCAL_BIN="/home/$USERNAME/.local/bin"
    41→mkdir -p "$USER_LOCAL_BIN"
    42→for tool in mise claude; do
    43→    if [ ! -f "$USER_LOCAL_BIN/$tool" ] && [ -f "/opt/sandcastle/bin/$tool" ]; then
    44→        cp "/opt/sandcastle/bin/$tool" "$USER_LOCAL_BIN/$tool"
    45→    fi
    46→done
    47→chown -R "$USERNAME:$USERNAME" "/home/$USERNAME/.local"
    48→
    49→# Configure git identity system-wide if provided
    50→if [ -n "$USER_FULLNAME" ] || [ -n "$USER_EMAIL" ]; then
    51→    {
    52→        echo "[user]"
    53→        [ -n "$USER_FULLNAME" ] && echo "    name = $USER_FULLNAME"
    54→        [ -n "$USER_EMAIL" ] && echo "    email = $USER_EMAIL"
    55→    } >> /etc/gitconfig
    56→fi
    57→
    58→# Generate SSH host keys if missing
    59→ssh-keygen -A
    60→
    61→# Start Docker daemon in background (requires Sysbox runtime for isolated /var/lib/docker)
    62→# Don't wait for it to be ready - users can check with `docker info` after SSH login
    63→if command -v dockerd &>/dev/null && [ -e /dev/fuse ]; then
    64→    # Match inner Docker bridge MTU to container's eth0 to avoid packet fragmentation
    65→    ETH0_MTU=$(ip link show eth0 2>/dev/null | grep -oP 'mtu \K[0-9]+' || echo 1500)
    66→    dockerd --storage-driver=overlay2 --mtu="$ETH0_MTU" &>/var/log/dockerd.log &
    67→else
    68→    echo "Note: Docker-in-Docker not available (requires sysbox-runc runtime)" >&2
    69→fi
    70→
    71→# Start virtual X server and VNC server for browser access
    72→if command -v Xvfb &>/dev/null && command -v x11vnc &>/dev/null; then
    73→    # Start Xvfb on display :99 with 1920x1080 resolution and 24-bit color depth
    74→    Xvfb :99 -screen 0 1920x1080x24 &>/var/log/xvfb.log &
    75→    # Wait a moment for Xvfb to initialize
    76→    sleep 1
    77→    # Start x11vnc server: -shared allows multiple connections, -forever keeps running,
    78→    # -nopw allows connections without password (secured via Docker network isolation)
    79→    DISPLAY=:99 x11vnc -shared -forever -nopw -rfbport 5900 &>/var/log/x11vnc.log &
    80→fi
    81→
    82→# Start SSH daemon in foreground
    83→exec /usr/sbin/sshd -D -e
    84→

85:  def ensure_mount_dirs(user, sandbox)
86-    # Directories bind-mounted into Sysbox containers must be world-writable
87-    # because Sysbox maps container root to a high host UID (via /etc/subuid)
88-    # that won't match the directory owner.
89-    if sandbox.mount_home
90-      dir = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/home"
91-      FileUtils.mkdir_p(dir)
92-      FileUtils.chmod(0o777, dir)
93-    end
94-    if sandbox.data_path.present?
95-      dir = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/data/#{sandbox.data_path}".chomp("/")
96-      FileUtils.mkdir_p(dir)
97-      FileUtils.chmod(0o777, dir)
98-    end
99-    if sandbox.persistent_volume && sandbox.volume_path
100-      FileUtils.mkdir_p(sandbox.volume_path)
101-      FileUtils.chmod(0o777, sandbox.volume_path)
102-    end
103-    # Chrome profile persistence: mount .config/google-chrome separately if not mounting full home
104-    if user.chrome_persist_profile? && !sandbox.mount_home
105-      dir = "#{DATA_DIR}/users/#{user.name}/chrome-profile"
106-      FileUtils.mkdir_p(dir)
107-      FileUtils.chmod(0o777, dir)
108-    end
109-  rescue Errno::EACCES, Errno::ENOENT => e
110-    raise Error, "Failed to create mount directories: #{e.message}"
