  "new_string": "```\n\n### Hot-Patching Files (Without Image Rebuild)\n\nFor quick fixes and debugging, you can patch Rails code and config files directly into running containers without rebuilding the Docker image.\n\n#### Common File Locations in Container\n\n- **Rails app code**: `/rails/app/`\n- **Services**: `/rails/app/services/`\n- **Controllers**: `/rails/app/controllers/`\n- **Models**: `/rails/app/models/`\n- **Config**: `/rails/config/`\n- **Database config**: `/rails/config/database.yml`\n- **Routes**: `/rails/config/routes.rb`\n- **Environment**: `/rails/.env` (not recommended, use docker-compose env vars instead)\n\n#### Patch a Single File\n\n```bash\n# From your local machine, copy a file to the running web container\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker cp /path/to/local/file.rb sandcastle-web:/rails/app/services/file.rb\"\n\n# Or from sandman host\nsudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker cp /tmp/sandbox_manager.rb sandcastle-web:/rails/app/services/sandbox_manager.rb\n\n# Restart web and worker to pick up changes (triggers bootsnap cache rebuild)\nsudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker compose -f /sandcastle/docker-compose.yml restart web worker\n```\n\n#### Patch Multiple Files\n\n```bash\n# From local machine: tar and pipe to container\ncd /Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle\ntar -czf - app/services/sandbox_manager.rb app/services/tailscale_manager.rb | \\\n  ssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec -i sandcastle-web tar -xzf - -C /rails/\"\n\n# Or: copy entire directory\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker cp app/services/. sandcastle-web:/rails/app/services/\"\n\n# Restart services\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker compose -f /sandcastle/docker-compose.yml restart web worker\"\n```\n\n#### Quick Patch Workflow\n\n```bash\n# 1. Edit file locally in your editor\nvim app/services/sandbox_manager.rb\n\n# 2. Copy to sandman\nscp app/services/sandbox_manager.rb hq.sandcastle.rocks:/tmp/\n\n# 3. Patch into container and restart\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"\n  sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker cp /tmp/sandbox_manager.rb sandcastle-web:/rails/app/services/sandbox_manager.rb &&\n  sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker compose -f /sandcastle/docker-compose.yml restart web worker\n\"\n\n# 4. Verify changes took effect\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec sandcastle-web grep -n 'DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE' /rails/app/services/tailscale_manager.rb\"\n```\n\n#### Patch Config Files\n\n```bash\n# Update database.yml\nscp config/database.yml hq.sandcastle.rocks:/tmp/\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"\n  sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker cp /tmp/database.yml sandcastle-web:/rails/config/database.yml &&\n  sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker compose -f /sandcastle/docker-compose.yml restart web worker\n\"\n\n# Update routes.rb\nscp config/routes.rb hq.sandcastle.rocks:/tmp/\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"\n  sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker cp /tmp/routes.rb sandcastle-web:/rails/config/routes.rb &&\n  sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker compose -f /sandcastle/docker-compose.yml restart web worker\n\"\n```\n\n#### Verify Patched Files\n\n```bash\n# Check file timestamp inside container\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec sandcastle-web ls -la /rails/app/services/sandbox_manager.rb\"\n\n# View file contents\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec sandcastle-web cat /rails/app/services/tailscale_manager.rb | grep -A5 'def subnet_for'\"\n\n# Check if changes are loaded (Rails console)\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec sandcastle-web bin/rails runner 'puts TailscaleManager.instance_methods(false)'\"\n```\n\n#### Rails Console for Live Debugging\n\n```bash\n# Open Rails console in web container\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec -it sandcastle-web bin/rails console\"\n\n# Run one-off commands\nssh hq.sandcastle.rocks \"sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec sandcastle-web bin/rails runner 'puts Sandbox.count'\"\n```\n\n#### Important Notes\n\n- **Temporary fixes**: Patched files are lost when containers are recreated (docker-compose up, pull, etc.)\n- **For permanent fixes**: Commit changes to git, push to GitHub, rebuild and redeploy the image\n- **Bootsnap cache**: Rails automatically rebuilds bootsnap cache on restart, no manual cache clearing needed\n- **Worker container**: Always restart both `web` and `worker` containers to keep code in sync\n- **File ownership**: Docker cp preserves file ownership; files will be owned by root inside container (this is fine)\n- **Zero-downtime**: Use `docker compose restart` instead of `down && up` to keep database running\n\n### Environment Variables"

   657→/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec sc-{sandbox-name} cat /home/{user}/.ssh/authorized_keys | grep -c "^ssh-rsa"
   658→```
   659→
   660→**Common causes:**
   661→- MTU mismatch: `entrypoint.sh` must detect eth0 MTU and configure dockerd
   662→- SSH key overwrite: Must use `>>` (append), not `>` (overwrite)
   663→- runc version: 1.2+ breaks Sysbox, must pin to 1.1.15
   664→
   665→#### 8. ContainerSyncJob Failures
   666→
   667→**Symptoms:** Sandbox status out of sync with Docker reality
   668→
   669→```bash
   670→# Check job failures
   671→docker-compose exec web bin/rails console
   672→> SolidQueue::FailedExecution.where(job_class: 'ContainerSyncJob').last(5).each do |f|
   673→>   puts "#{f.created_at}: #{f.error}"
   674→> end
   675→
   676→# Run manual sync
   677→> SandboxManager.new.sync_all_containers
   678→
   679→# Check Solid Queue logs
   680→docker-compose logs web | grep ContainerSyncJob
   681→```
   682→
   683→**Resolution:**
   684→- Ensure Dockyard socket accessible from app container
   685→- Check for stale container IDs in database
   686→- Verify `docker-api` gem version compatibility
   687→
   688→### Rails Console Debugging
   689→
   690→```bash
   691→docker-compose exec web bin/rails console
   692→
   693→# Force sync sandbox state
   694→> sb = Sandbox.find_by(name: 'test-sandbox')
   695→> SandboxManager.new.sync_container(sb)
   696→
   697→# Check Docker API connectivity
   698→> require 'docker'
   699→> Docker.url

Perfect! I've updated SANDMAN.md with comprehensive hot-patching instructions. Here's what I added:

## New Section: Hot-Patching Files (Without Image Rebuild)

Added complete documentation including:

### 📁 Common File Locations
- Rails app paths inside containers (`/rails/app/`, `/rails/config/`, etc.)

### 🔧 Patching Workflows

**Single file:**
```bash
scp app/services/sandbox_manager.rb hq.sandcastle.rocks:/tmp/
ssh hq.sandcastle.rocks "
  sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker cp /tmp/sandbox_manager.rb sandcastle-web:/rails/app/services/sandbox_manager.rb &&
  sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker compose -f /sandcastle/docker-compose.yml restart web worker
"
```

**Multiple files:**
```bash
tar -czf - app/services/*.rb | \
  ssh hq.sandcastle.rocks "sudo /sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker exec -i sandcastle-web tar -xzf - -C /rails/"
```

### ✅ Verification Methods
- Check file timestamps
- View patched content
- Test via Rails console

### ⚠️ Important Notes
- Patches are temporary (lost on container recreate)
- Always restart both `web` and `worker` containers
- Bootsnap cache rebuilds automatically
- For permanent fixes: commit → push → rebuild image

### 📍 Also Added To
- **Quick Reference section** - One-liner for fast patching
- **Changelog** - Documented the update

The documentation is now complete for quick fixes on hq.sandcastle.rocks without full redeployments!

Perfect! The hot-patch verification confirms the fix is working:

## ✅ API Connect Endpoint Fixed

**Before patch:**
```
→ POST /api/sandboxes/1/connect
← 404 (21 bytes)
  response: {"error":"Not found"}
```

**After patch:**
```
→ POST /api/sandboxes/4/connect
← 200 (107 bytes) ✅
  response: {"host":"hq.sandcastle.rocks","port":2201,"user":"thies","command":"ssh -p 2201 thies@hq.sandcastle.rocks"}
```

### What Was Fixed

1. **Tailscale subnet alignment** - Now uses `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=172.89.0.0/16` instead of hardcoded `172.30.99.0/24`
2. **Better API error messages** - Detailed diagnostics when sandbox can't be found
3. **Connect endpoint** - Returns proper connection info instead of 404

### Remaining Issue (Separate from API Fix)

The SSH timeout is due to sandbox container initialization problems (permission denied creating subdirectories), NOT the API endpoint. The API is now working correctly - it successfully:
- ✅ Creates sandbox (201)
- ✅ Returns connect info (200)
- ✅ Provisions container (stopped state, not destroyed)

The container just can't start SSH because the entrypoint script hits permission errors when trying to create directories inside the bind-mounted home directory.

**Summary:** The original issue you reported (`sandcastle create` returning 404 on connect) is **completely fixed** with the hot-patch! 🎉

  def subnet_for(user)
    base = ENV.fetch("DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE", "172.89.0.0/16")
    # Derive per-user /24 from the /16 pool allocated by dockyard
    # e.g. base 172.89.0.0/16, user_id 1 → 172.89.1.0/24, user_id 2 → 172.89.2.0/24
    # This ensures Tailscale networks are within DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE so dockyard's NAT rules apply
    parts = base.split("/").first.split(".").map(&:to_i)
```

> AGENT

Perfect! I found the root cause. The error is:
```
store.New failed: creating state directory: chmod /var/lib/tailscale: value too large for defined data type
```

This is a Sysbox bind mount issue. The solution is to use a named Docker volume instead of a bind mount for the Tailscale state. Let me recreate the container properly:

> TOOL

}
```

> AGENT

## ✅ Tailscale Routing Fixed!

The Tailscale container now has full network connectivity. Here's what was fixed:

### Root Causes
1. **Old subnet** - Network was using `172.30.101.0/24` (outside NAT coverage)
2. **Missing env var** - `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE` wasn't in Rails containers
3. **Bind mount issue** - Sysbox can't handle chmod on bind-mounted `/var/lib/tailscale`

### Solution Applied

1. **Added DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE to environment**
   ```yaml
   # docker-compose.yml
   DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE: ${DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE:-172.89.0.0/16}
   ```

2. **Recreated network with correct subnet**
   ```bash
   # Old: 172.30.101.0/24 (no NAT) ❌
   # New: 172.89.1.0/24 (within 172.89.0.0/16 NAT) ✅
   ```

3. **Used named volume instead of bind mount**
   ```bash
   # Old: -v /sandcastle/data/users/thies/tailscale:/var/lib/tailscale ❌
   # New: -v sc-ts-thies-state:/var/lib/tailscale ✅
   ```

## ✅ Complete Fix Summary

### What We Fixed

1. **Tailscale Network Connectivity** ✅
   - **Before:** `172.30.101.0/24` (no NAT rules, no internet)
   - **After:** `172.89.1.0/24` (within `172.89.0.0/16` NAT coverage)
   - **Result:** Full internet connectivity confirmed

2. **Environment Configuration** ✅
   - Added `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=172.89.0.0/16` to `/sandcastle/.env`
   - Added to `docker-compose.yml` for web/worker containers
   - Rails now uses correct subnet for new Tailscale networks

3. **Storage Issue** ✅
   - **Before:** Bind mount to `/sandcastle/data/users/thies/tailscale` (chmod fails in Sysbox)
   - **After:** Named Docker volume `sc-ts-thies-state` (works perfectly)
   - **Result:** No more "state store is unhealthy" errors

### Current Status

**Tailscale Container:**
- ✅ Running on network `sc-ts-net-thies` (`172.89.1.0/24`)
- ✅ Full internet connectivity (ping, DNS working)
- ✅ NAT rules applied (`172.89.0.0/16` → MASQUERADE)
- ⏳ Awaiting authentication

**Authentication URL:**
```
https://login.tailscale.com/a/1e366ee901d510
```

Visit this URL to complete authentication and bring the Tailscale network online!

### Files Changed on Sandman
- `/sandcastle/.env` - Added `DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=172.89.0.0/16`
- `/sandcastle/docker-compose.yml` - Added DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE env var to services

All fixes are now live on sandman! 🎉

- Ensures Tailscale networks fall within `172.89.0.0/16` NAT coverage
- Fixed connectivity for Tailscale containers
